Every assignment names its baseline and target worktree. QA validates the integrated candidate, not different roles' unrelated snapshots.

## Roles — one role, one task

| Code | Role / task title | Responsibility | Phase 1 writing boundary after START and assignment |
|---|---|---|---|
| PM | NQR — Project Manager | Single user contact; backlog, scheduling, ownership, blocker routing, acceptance package | This plan, task registry, work-item ledger and coordination reports |
| PRODUCT | NQR — Product Manager | Scope, acceptance criteria, user decisions, UAT checklist | Product reports; no authority to approve launch or new scope |
| TL | NQR — Tech Lead | Architecture, baseline, integration, independent code review, shared contracts | Shared changes only by explicit PM assignment; package files, i18n contract, domain contracts |
| DS | NQR — UX/UI Designer | Design consistency, accessibility and interaction states | UI primitives, globals.css and layouts as assigned; no broad redesign |
| RENDER | NQR — QR Rendering Engineer | QR rendering, scan safeguards, PNG/SVG/PDF export | src/qr/render/** |
| FORMS | NQR — Frontend Developer | Generator forms, validation UI, preview, locale home | src/components/generator/** and src/app/[locale]/page.tsx |
| SEO | NQR — SEO & i18n Engineer | Landing pages, metadata, canonical/hreflang, sitemap and robots | src/seo/**, src/app/[locale]/qr/**, sitemap.ts, robots.ts |
| QA | NQR — QA Engineer | Independent tests, browser E2E, keyboard/accessibility, regression and release evidence | Verification reports and explicitly assigned tests; not all test files by default |
| DEVOPS | NQR — DevOps Engineer | Origin gate, CI, migration drift check, release documentation, bundle audit | .github/**, next.config.ts, .env.example, README.md and assigned scripts |
| SECURITY | NQR — Security Engineer | Privacy and security review, risk triage, regression requirements | Read-only review and reports; fixes routed to code owner |
| BACKEND | NQR — Backend Developer | Read-only database/schema review and advice to drift-check owner | No schema/migration/API change in Phase 1; Phase 2 stays parked |

This extends the historical TEAM.md roles without changing its Phase 1 product constraints. PM remains accountable for shared contracts; TL implements them only under PM assignment. File ownership is not blanket authorization to edit during standby.

## Onboarding contract

Initial read-only onboarding is complete. For every new assignment read the current source ENGINEERING_LOOP.md and TEAM_REPORTING.md, not only the older snapshot copies. Write the assigned result, then send one DONE/BLOCKED/REVIEW_FAILED report directly to PM before ending your turn. Do not create more tasks/agents/automations or self-assign work. Earlier instructions prohibiting cross-task reporting no longer apply to reports sent to PM.

Use only the assigned role's perspective. A role that has no current Phase 1 implementation work remains parked, rather than inventing features. No task is a perpetual process; it resumes only on an assignment. PM's heartbeat is the single recurring coordinator.

## Work plan after START

1. **Baseline and acceptance:** PRODUCT clarifies acceptance and missing product decisions; TL resolves baseline and integration; QA establishes a fresh test baseline. No implementation on stale worktrees.
2. **Release hardening:** DEVOPS starts the origin misconfiguration gate, then CI, migration drift and release docs. SEO advises on origin policy; BACKEND reviews drift-check design without requiring a live DB; TL handles approved shared package changes. SECURITY reviews the browser-only privacy boundary.
3. **Runtime QA:** QA drives the real browser for all ten QR types, Thai/English routes, validation, live preview, logo and quality rules, exports, keyboard access and responsive layouts. DS, FORMS and RENDER receive targeted defects. Run resource-conflicting browser/build checks sequentially.
4. **Repair loop:** assigned owner fixes a reproducible defect, TL reviews the change, QA retests and adds suitable regression coverage. Findings return to the owner until verified; failed tests are not waived by the implementer.
5. **Release candidate:** validate the same integrated baseline with typecheck, lint, tests and build; inspect bundle and origin outputs. DEVOPS supplies a release/rollback checklist without deploying.
6. **User handoff:** PM delivers artifacts, changed-file summary, verification evidence, known issues, operational instructions and the product-owned gates. User accepts or requests revisions. Revisions restart the loop inside the agreed scope.

## Engineering loop and dispatch rules

`PLAN → READY → IN_PROGRESS → REVIEW → QA → VERIFIED → HANDOFF → ACCEPTED`

`REVIEW or QA failure → REWORK → REVIEW → QA`

`Missing user authority / material decision → NEEDS_USER → resume only after a response`

- Each work item has id, owner, scope, permitted files, baseline/candidate, dependencies, acceptance tests, reviewer, status and evidence.
- At most three role tasks run concurrently; avoid overlapping writers even across worktrees because changes later need integration. PM is coordinator, not another uncontrolled writer.
- Before dispatch, inspect current task status and ledger. Never dispatch the same open assignment twice or send new work to a running role without a justified change in scope.
- Use existing task ids and bounded wait snapshots. Worktree setup failure is reported and resolved, not silently replaced with duplicate tasks.
- Each loop advances only actionable work. If nothing changed, avoid repetitive reports. If a fix attempt repeats the same failure three times, TL changes the approach; escalation to the user is reserved for actual missing authority, decisions or external prerequisites.
- Do not add dependencies, alter frozen contracts or reassign another role's files without PM authorization. User permission and environment approvals still apply.
- Do not add Phase 2/3 features, publish a site, transfer money, buy a domain, apply production migrations or modify real production settings without explicit user authorization.
- Check only in-scope source and development resources. Do not read or copy secret .env values into reports or new worktrees.

Other review gaps are not waived: UTF-8 capacity/overflow recovery and ECI/scanner interpretation; bare-CR/control-character payload semantics; full network/storage/browser coverage. They require bounded follow-up/QA on the assembled candidate, not unapproved Phase 2 work. No automatic payload exfiltration was proven by the source review.

Control-character triage from NQR-031: CTRL-001 LOW is accepted for a narrow shared encoder fix: CRLF is one logical newline and lone CR/LF are escaped in text-valued vCard/Event properties, keeping existing ordinary/LF/CRLF/delimiter behavior and structural separators. URI-valued website behavior is explicitly excluded pending URL policy; no blanket normalization. CTRL-002 LOW is accepted for form coercion repair: preserve exact raw password when not blank, retain current empty/whitespace-only rejection, existing schema length constraints and nopass omission. Do not silently trim a credential to another value or broaden blank/control acceptance. URL parser-vs-wire mismatch, wider NUL/DEL/phone/subject/credential-control policy and actual scanner/native-control semantics remain OPEN, not demonstrated exploits or waived risks. Details: `docs/reports/PM_CONTROL_CHARACTER_TRIAGE.md`.

Capacity triage from NQR-030: QA-CAP-001 P2 REQUEST_CHANGES accepted for safe localized capacity-specific feedback and preventing TestScan/export for confirmed current overflow; preserve pending/current revision distinctions, full supported modes/ECC and payload bytes, with no blanket character-limit reduction. QA-CAP-002 P3 accepted for reusable renderer fail-closed raw-output state after failed updates; current UI fresh-render exports did not demonstrate stale saves. NQR-034 implements only the API state guard and proposes the capacity/UI contract; no capacity/UI implementation until that plan is reviewed. Details/reproduced evidence: `docs/reports/PM_CAPACITY_TRIAGE.md`. Actual artifact/ECI/browser acceptance remains pending.

## Verification and acceptance

- All ten content types function; unsupported/bad input is handled in the correct locale.
- Rendering and PNG 512/1024/2048, SVG and PDF exports are verified; quiet zone, logo/ECC rules and download blocking stay enforced.
- Thai and English routes, metadata and origin outputs are checked.
- QR payloads never leave the browser during generation/export; no new server round-trip for generation.
- Record actual `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm test`, `npm run build` results on the integrated candidate. Historical results are not current evidence.
- Browser QA must identify tested browser, scenarios and evidence. Keyboard and screen-reader checks must distinguish actual tests from inferred accessibility; untested claims stay explicit.
- No open release-blocking defect. Other known issues have severity, impact and an explicit acceptance or deferral decision.
- No engineer self-approves their own fixes as final QA.
- Engineering handoff can be ready while launch is blocked; label this distinction clearly. If any engineering acceptance criterion is unverified, report a conditional handoff rather than calling it complete.

### Gates only the user can resolve

1. Validate a real PromptPay-linked account with at least three Thai bank apps, verify displayed payee and amount, then cancel before transfer. Repo tests cannot replace this.
2. Select the production domain and ensure NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL is configured in the authorized production environment.
3. Approve any deployment, paid service, real credentials/access, new scope, or change to the existing uncommitted baseline that needs new authority.
4. Accept the deliverable. Short-domain registration remains a Phase 2 gate, not a reason to expand Phase 1.

## Who speaks with the user

**Project Manager is the primary contact in this task.** The user does not need to relay messages between specialists.

- Product questions and UAT: PRODUCT prepares a short decision brief; PM brings it to the user.
- Architecture/cost/risk tradeoffs: TL supplies options and impact; PM asks the user only when the decision affects agreed scope or authority.
- Domain/deployment/access: DEVOPS defines the exact needed action; PM requests it without asking for secrets in chat.
- Bank-app testing and acceptance: PM coordinates with the user; no agent impersonates real-world verification.

Status format: completed / in progress / next / blockers / decision needed from you. Bring specialist tasks into the conversation only when useful; the user can contact any role directly if desired.

## Automation

Automation id: `nqr-engineering-loop` — PAUSED while canonical origin/build inputs are missing. Resume only after those inputs arrive; do not create a replacement automation.

Use a single thread heartbeat attached to the PM task. It starts PAUSED. On explicit START, PM updates control state and activates the existing heartbeat; do not create duplicates. Cadence is a fallback coordination check, not a promise that work runs while the app/machine is unavailable.

On each direct callback or backup heartbeat: read this control state, TEAM_REPORTING.md and latest user messages; deduplicate report identities; collect task status using saved ids/cursors; verify evidence; dispatch the next authorized non-overlapping assignment immediately. No implementation is allowed while state is STANDBY. A worker callback is evidence, not new user authority. Do not create ACK ping-pong.

On engineering handoff delivery, pause the heartbeat and enter AWAITING_ACCEPTANCE. On user acceptance, enter ACCEPTED. On a genuine user-input blocker, pause the heartbeat and enter NEEDS_USER after exhausting safe in-scope checks. Preserve pending work and request one concrete decision; do not repeatedly wake with the same question. On user pause/stop, pause immediately. Revisions resume the same tasks and heartbeat when authorized.

Reference consulted for the scheduling mechanism: [OpenAI Docs — Scheduled tasks](https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/automations?surface=app).
